==============================================================================
SUMMARY 3. P2 counterfactual shared judge pool, multi-server overlay, PRIMARY trace
==============================================================================
The original platform had one Docker container per student and no shared queue; the traces
keep the real arrival times (I-res on the jittered clock: ts + u - C) and costs; the pool, queue
and order are ours.  Primary pool: ['2020-ERE', '2020-2', '2021-1', '2021-2', '2022-1', '2022-2'] (40 class-
semesters), 44 overlay copies, each class-semester copy week-aligned by weekday and clock with
its own 0-11 whole-week jitter; one overlay per rep (seed 300 + rep); k per level from that
overlay's busiest-hour C_cap work.  Primary metric: deadline-window p99 wait (p99_dl: arrivals in
the 24 h before the assessment end, research_plan.md 6.5), then mean wait, overall p99 and the
heavy-job waits (heavy = TRUE C_cap > 1.559 s, a reporting label only).  gain = (W_FCFS - W_policy)
/ (W_FCFS - W_SJF-ref) per overlay, then averaged; SJF-ref = non-preemptive SJF on the TRUE C_cap, a
reference, not a bound.  [CI] = 95% paired week-block bootstrap (2,000 resamples of whole weeks of
the overlay timeline, the same draws for every policy, level and overlay).  Overall-p99 gains are
point values (no CI).  Predictions: forward (rolling-origin) LightGBM regression per target semester.

--- 3f. v1 published vs v2.  DIFFERENT POOLS AND LOADS: v1 = 77 class-semesters (2018-1..2022-2)
    x 3 copies, out-of-fold predictions, leaky v1 features, arrival = whole-second ts; v2 primary =
    40 class-semesters of the 60 s semesters x 44 copies; v2 pool19 = 49 class-semesters incl. 2019-1 x 8 copies;

==============================================================================
P1c. FORWARD (ROLLING-ORIGIN) PREDICTIONS FOR THE SIMULATION
==============================================================================
for each target semester s from 2019-1 on (calendar order of first events): train on
the submissions of the semesters that start before s, minus every record whose
assumed availability time (done + delta) is at or after the first event of s;
LightGBM regression as v1, seed 3.  Replaces v1's out-of-fold predictions.
The heavy cut-off and user-slowness tercile cuts INSIDE the features (heavy-rate and
similar-user columns) are the fixed 2018-1..2019-2 values only when all of those rows are
training rows of the target; otherwise (targets 2019-1, 2019-2) they are refit on the
target's own training rows and every feature is rebuilt with them (cutoffs column).  The
heavy LABEL used for evaluation stays the fixed train p95 (research_plan.md 6.1).
slow_train / slow_target = rows arriving 2019-06-01..2019-09-30 (the platform-wide slow
period: median C of trivial correct runs 0.73-1.06 s vs 0.15-0.23 s elsewhere).

assumed availability time (done + delta) is at or after the first event of s;
LightGBM regression as v1, seed 3.  Replaces v1's out-of-fold predictions.
The heavy cut-off and user-slowness tercile cuts INSIDE the features (heavy-rate and
similar-user columns) are the fixed 2018-1..2019-2 values only when all of those rows are
training rows of the target; otherwise (targets 2019-1, 2019-2) they are refit on the
target's own training rows and every feature is rebuilt with them (cutoffs column).  The
heavy LABEL used for evaluation stays the fixed train p95 (research_plan.md 6.1).
slow_train / slow_target = rows arriving 2019-06-01..2019-09-30 (the platform-wide slow
period: median C of trivial correct runs 0.73-1.06 s vs 0.15-0.23 s elsewhere).

==============================================================================
P2b. WEEK-BLOCK BOOTSTRAP (2,000 resamples, paired over policies, levels and overlays)
==============================================================================
A resample draws whole weeks of the overlay timeline with replacement (the trace's week set,
p2weeks.csv); every policy, level and overlay rep of a trace uses the same draws.  Per resample:
gain_r = (FCFS_r - policy_r) / (FCFS_r - SJF-ref_r) on overlay r, averaged over the reps; the
point gain uses the full trace.  CI = 2.5/97.5% of the resamples.  unstable = rep-mean
(FCFS - SJF-ref) < 0.1 s or < 5% of rep-mean FCFS on that metric.  A pairwise difference is
gain(a) - gain(b); 'resolved' when its CI excludes 0.

  [     1s] isub0 (all, reps [0, 1, 2]) done
